- **index**: Antiphon 通过与本地 `antiphond` 守护进程通信的小型适配器来聆听智能体会话。每个适配器都是**故障即放行（fail-open）**的：如果 Antiphon 未安装或未运行，你的智能体的行为与插件不存在时完全一致。会话绝不会因守护进程而阻塞。 ([cfoust/antiphon](https://github.com/cfoust/antiphon/blob/HEAD/docs/i18n/zh-Hans/docusaurus-plugin-content-docs/current/agents/index.md) · ★1)
- **index**: Antiphon 透過小巧的轉接器聆聽代理工作階段，這些轉接器與本機的 `antiphond` 常駐程式溝通。每個轉接器都是 **fail-open**：如果沒有安裝或沒有執行 Antiphon，你的代理的行為就跟外掛不存在時一模一樣。工作階段永遠不會因為常駐程式而被卡住。 ([cfoust/antiphon](https://github.com/cfoust/antiphon/blob/HEAD/docs/i18n/zh-Hant/docusaurus-plugin-content-docs/current/agents/index.md) · ★1)
- **index**: Antiphon listens to agent sessions through small adapters that talk to the local `antiphond` daemon. Every adapter is **fail-open**: if Antiphon isn't installed or isn't running, your agent behaves exactly as if the plugin didn't exist. Nothing ever blocks a session on the daemon. ([cfoust/antiphon](https://github.com/cfoust/antiphon/blob/HEAD/docs/docs/agents/index.md) · ★1)
- **index**: Antiphon слушает сессии агентов через небольшие адаптеры, которые общаются с локальным демоном `antiphond`. Каждый адаптер — **fail-open**: если Antiphon не установлен или не запущен, агент ведёт себя ровно так, как будто плагина не существует. Ничто и никогда не блокирует сессию из-за демона. ([cfoust/antiphon](https://github.com/cfoust/antiphon/blob/HEAD/docs/i18n/ru/docusaurus-plugin-content-docs/current/agents/index.md) · ★1)

- **infra-planner**: Infra runbook planner for JP's projects — the solutions-architect of the infra track. Runs on issues labelled `infra` (DNS, Vercel domains/env/redirects, Supabase auth config, webhooks, API keys, CI workflow config, credentials rotation — anything that changes a running system without a feature PR). Inventories current state with read-only commands, then posts an executable runbook on the issue: per step, exact command, expected output, verification, rollback, environment. Never runs a mutating command. ([jpmoya/claude-agents](https://github.com/jpmoya/claude-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/infra-planner.md))
- **infra-platform**: You own the **foundation**: the monorepo, the database, authentication, the household model, queues, the worker runtime, and the Nango deployment. Every other specialist builds on what you ship. ([cnnrobrn/homehub](https://github.com/cnnrobrn/homehub/blob/HEAD/scripts/agents/infra-platform.md))
